Text Vectorization for Categories\n```python\nfrom sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Vectorize category names for similarity computation\nvectorizer = TfidfVectorizer(\n    ngram_range=(1, 2),\n    lowercase=True,\n    stop_words='english'\n)\n\n# Fit on all category names\nall_categories = df['category_name'].unique()\ntfidf_matrix = vectorizer.fit_transform(all_categories)\n```\n\n### 2. Computing Text Similarity\n```python\nfrom sklearn.metrics.pairwise import cosine_similarity\n\n# Compute pairwise similarity\nsimilarity_matrix = cosine_similarity(tfidf_matrix)\n\n# Find similar categories (threshold-based)\ndef find_similar_categories(idx, threshold=0.7):\n    similar_indices = np.where(similarity_matrix[idx] >= threshold)[0]\n    return [all_categories[i] for i in similar_indices if i != idx]\n```\n\n### 3. Fuzzy String Matching\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import fuzz, process\n\ndef fuzzy_match_category(name, candidates, threshold=80):\n    \"\"\"Find best matching category using fuzzy matching.\"\"\"\n    result = process.extractOne(\n        name,\n        candidates,\n        scorer=fuzz.token_sort_ratio\n    )\n    if result and result[1] >= threshold:\n        return result[0]\n    return None\n```\n\n### 4. Hierarchical Clustering\n```python\nfrom sklearn.cluster import AgglomerativeClustering\nfrom scipy.cluster.hierarchy import dendrogram, linkage\n\n# Cluster categories hierarchically\ndef cluster_categories(tfidf_matrix, n_clusters):\n    \"\"\"Cluster category names into groups.\"\"\"\n    clustering = AgglomerativeClustering(\n        n_clusters=n_clusters,\n        metric='cosine',\n        linkage='average'\n    )\n    labels = clustering.fit_predict(tfidf_matrix.toarray())\n    return labels\n\n# Alternative: Use linkage for more control\nlinkage_matrix = linkage(tfidf_matrix.toarray(), method='ward')\n```\n\n### 5. Word Overlap Calculation\n```python\ndef word_overlap_ratio(text1, text2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate word overlap between two strings.\"\"\"\n    words1 = set(text1.lower().split())\n    words2 = set(text2.lower().split())\n\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = words1 & words2\n    min_len = min(len(words1), len(words2))\n\n    return len(intersection) / min_len\n\n# Check sibling categories have < 30% overlap\ndef validate_sibling_overlap(siblings, max_overlap=0.3):\n    for i, cat1 in enumerate(siblings):\n        for cat2 in siblings[i+1:]:\n            if word_overlap_ratio(cat1, cat2) > max_overlap:\n                return False, (cat1, cat2)\n    return True, None\n```\n\n### 6. Representative Name Selection (70%+ coverage)\n```python\nfrom collections import Counter\n\ndef select_representative_name(categories, min_coverage=0.7):\n    \"\"\"Select a name that represents 70%+ of subcategories.\"\"\"\n    # Tokenize all categories\n    all_words = []\n    for cat in categories:\n        all_words.extend(cat.lower().split())\n\n    word_counts = Counter(all_words)\n    total = len(categories)\n\n    # Find words appearing in 70%+ of categories\n    representative_words = [\n        word for word, count in word_counts.items()\n        if count / total >= min_coverage\n    ]\n\n    return ' | '.join(representative_words[:5])  # Max 5 words\n```\n\n### 7. Taxonomy Merging Strategy\n```python\ndef merge_taxonomies(sources):\n    \"\"\"Merge category taxonomies from multiple sources.\"\"\"\n    # Step 1: Collect all unique top-level categories\n    all_top_level = set()\n    for source_df in sources:\n        all_top_level.update(source_df['level_1'].unique())\n\n    # Step 2: Cluster similar top-level categories\n    # Step 3: Select representative names\n    # Step 4: Recursively merge subcategories\n\n    return unified_taxonomy\n```\n\n## Validation Rules\n\n### Parent-Child Name Validation\n```python\ndef validate_no_parent_overlap(parent, child):\n    \"\"\"Ensure child name doesn't overlap with parent.\"\"\"\n    parent_words = set(parent.lower().split())\n    child_words = set(child.lower().split())\n    return not parent_words.intersection(child_words)\n```\n\n### Balanced Hierarchy Check\n```python\ndef check_balanced_hierarchy(taxonomy_df, min_children=3, max_children=20):\n    \"\"\"Check if hierarchy is balanced.\"\"\"\n    for level in range(1, 5):\n        parent_col = f'level_{level}'\n        child_col = f'level_{level+1}'\n\n        child_counts = taxonomy_df.groupby(parent_col)[child_col].nunique()\n\n        # Check bounds\n        if child_counts.min() < min_children or child_counts.max() > max_children:\n            return False\n    return True\n```\n\n## Output Format\nThe unified taxonomy should have clear level separators and consistent naming:\n```\nLevel 1: Broad categories (10-20)\nLevel 2: Main subcategories (3-20 per parent)\nLevel 3: Specific subcategories\nLevel 4: Detailed categories\nLevel 5: Most specific categories\n```\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":940,"cache_read_input_tokens":27393,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":940,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"cd7bc42e-d3aa-448c-a0c6-ed8448ad6d7a","uuid":"af830bf5-7ca4-4d40-a559-780145399355"}

Hierarchical Clustering\n```python\nfrom sklearn.cluster import AgglomerativeClustering\nfrom scipy.cluster.hierarchy import dendrogram, linkage\n\n# Cluster categories hierarchically\ndef cluster_categories(tfidf_matrix, n_clusters):\n    \"\"\"Cluster category names into groups.\"\"\"\n    clustering = AgglomerativeClustering(\n        n_clusters=n_clusters,\n        metric='cosine',\n        linkage='average'\n    )\n    labels = clustering.fit_predict(tfidf_matrix.toarray())\n    return labels\n\n# Alternative: Use linkage for more control\nlinkage_matrix = linkage(tfidf_matrix.toarray(), method='ward')\n```\n\n### 5. Word Overlap Calculation\n```python\ndef word_overlap_ratio(text1, text2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate word overlap between two strings.\"\"\"\n    words1 = set(text1.lower().split())\n    words2 = set(text2.lower().split())\n\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = words1 & words2\n    min_len = min(len(words1), len(words2))\n\n    return len(intersection) / min_len\n\n# Check sibling categories have < 30% overlap\ndef validate_sibling_overlap(siblings, max_overlap=0.3):\n    for i, cat1 in enumerate(siblings):\n        for cat2 in siblings[i+1:]:\n            if word_overlap_ratio(cat1, cat2) > max_overlap:\n                return False, (cat1, cat2)\n    return True, None\n```\n\n### 6. Representative Name Selection (70%+ coverage)\n```python\nfrom collections import Counter\n\ndef select_representative_name(categories, min_coverage=0.7):\n    \"\"\"Select a name that represents 70%+ of subcategories.\"\"\"\n    # Tokenize all categories\n    all_words = []\n    for cat in categories:\n        all_words.extend(cat.lower().split())\n\n    word_counts = Counter(all_words)\n    total = len(categories)\n\n    # Find words appearing in 70%+ of categories\n    representative_words = [\n        word for word, count in word_counts.items()\n        if count / total >= min_coverage\n    ]\n\n    return ' | '.join(representative_words[:5])  # Max 5 words\n```\n\n### 7. Taxonomy Merging Strategy\n```python\ndef merge_taxonomies(sources):\n    \"\"\"Merge category taxonomies from multiple sources.\"\"\"\n    # Step 1: Collect all unique top-level categories\n    all_top_level = set()\n    for source_df in sources:\n        all_top_level.update(source_df['level_1'].unique())\n\n    # Step 2: Cluster similar top-level categories\n    # Step 3: Select representative names\n    # Step 4: Recursively merge subcategories\n\n    return unified_taxonomy\n```\n\n## Validation Rules\n\n### Parent-Child Name Validation\n```python\ndef validate_no_parent_overlap(parent, child):\n    \"\"\"Ensure child name doesn't overlap with parent.\"\"\"\n    parent_words = set(parent.lower().split())\n    child_words = set(child.lower().split())\n    return not parent_words.intersection(child_words)\n```\n\n### Balanced Hierarchy Check\n```python\ndef check_balanced_hierarchy(taxonomy_df, min_children=3, max_children=20):\n    \"\"\"Check if hierarchy is balanced.\"\"\"\n    for level in range(1, 5):\n        parent_col = f'level_{level}'\n        child_col = f'level_{level+1}'\n\n        child_counts = taxonomy_df.groupby(parent_col)[child_col].nunique()\n\n        # Check bounds\n        if child_counts.min() < min_children or child_counts.max() > max_children:\n            return False\n    return True\n```\n\n## Output Format\nThe unified taxonomy should have clear level separators and consistent naming:\n```\nLevel 1: Broad categories (10-20)\nLevel 2: Main subcategories (3-20 per parent)\nLevel 3: Specific subcategories\nLevel 4: Detailed categories\nLevel 5: Most specific categories\n```\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Text Normalization\n```python\nimport re\n\ndef normalize_category_text(text):\n    \"\"\"Normalize category text for consistency.\"\"\"\n    if not text or pd.isna(text):\n        return \"\"\n\n    # Convert to lowercase first\n    text = str(text).lower()\n\n    # Remove quotes\n    text = text.strip('\"\\'')\n\n    # Normalize whitespace\n    text = ' '.join(text.split())\n\n    # Standardize common variations\n    replacements = {\n        '&amp;': '&',\n        ' and ': ' & ',\n        ' n ': ' & ',\n        \"'s\": 's',\n        '-': ' ',\n        '_': ' ',\n    }\n    for old, new in replacements.items():\n        text = text.replace(old, new)\n\n    return text.strip()\n```\n\n### 2. Pyramid Structure\nA well-designed taxonomy should form a pyramid:\n- Level 1: 10-20 broad categories (widest base in terms of coverage)\n- Level 2: 3-20 subcategories per Level 1\n- Level 3: 3-20 subcategories per Level 2\n- Level 4: 3-20 subcategories per Level 3\n- Level 5: 3-20 leaf categories per Level 4 (most specific)\n\n### 2. Balanced Distribution\n```python\ndef calculate_balance_score(taxonomy_df, level_col):\n    \"\"\"Calculate how balanced the subcategories are under a parent.\"\"\"\n    counts = taxonomy_df.groupby(level_col).size()\n\n    # Coefficient of variation (lower is more balanced)\n    cv = counts.std() / counts.mean()\n\n    # Ideal range check\n    in_range = (counts >= 3) & (counts <= 20)\n    range_compliance = in_range.mean()\n\n    return {'cv': cv, 'range_compliance': range_compliance}\n```\n\n### 3. Source Distribution Across Taxonomy\n```python\ndef check_source_distribution(taxonomy_df, source_col, level_col):\n    \"\"\"Ensure categories from all sources are evenly distributed.\"\"\"\n    source_counts = taxonomy_df.groupby(level_col)[source_col].value_counts()\n    source_coverage = taxonomy_df.groupby(level_col)[source_col].nunique()\n\n    # Ideal: all 3 sources represented in most categories\n    good_coverage = (source_coverage >= 2).mean()\n    return good_coverage\n```\n\n## Implementation Techniques\n\n### 4. Subcategory Clustering\n```python\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\ndef cluster_into_subcategories(items, min_size=3, max_size=20):\n    \"\"\"Cluster items into balanced subcategories.\"\"\"\n    n_items = len(items)\n\n    if n_items <= max_size:\n        # No clustering needed, items fit in one group\n        return {'General': items}\n\n    # Target number of clusters\n    target_clusters = max(n_items // 10, 3)  # Aim for ~10 items per cluster\n\n    # Use first word as initial grouping\n    groups = defaultdict(list)\n    for item in items:\n        first_word = item.split()[0] if item.split() else 'Other'\n        groups[first_word].append(item)\n\n    # Merge small groups\n    merged = {}\n    small_items = []\n    for name, group_items in groups.items():\n        if len(group_items) < min_size:\n            small_items.extend(group_items)\n        else:\n            merged[name] = group_items\n\n    if small_items:\n        merged['Other'] = small_items\n\n    return merged\n```\n\n### 6. Depth Adjustment\n```python\ndef adjust_depth(path_parts, target_depth=5):\n    \"\"\"Adjust category path to target depth.\"\"\"\n    current_depth = len(path_parts)\n\n    if current_depth == target_depth:\n        return path_parts\n\n    if current_depth > target_depth:\n        # Truncate to target depth\n        return path_parts[:target_depth]\n\n    if current_depth < target_depth:\n        # Pad with None for missing levels\n        return path_parts + [None] * (target_depth - current_depth)\n```\n\n### 7. Hierarchy Validation\n```python\ndef validate_hierarchy(taxonomy_df):\n    \"\"\"Validate the complete taxonomy hierarchy.\"\"\"\n    issues = []\n\n    # Check Level 1 count\n    l1_count = taxonomy_df['unified_level_1'].nunique()\n    if not (10 <= l1_count <= 20):\n        issues.append(f\"Level 1 has {l1_count} categories (should be 10-20)\")\n\n    # Check subcategory counts at each level\n    for level in range(1, 5):\n        parent_col = f'unified_level_{level}'\n        child_col = f'unified_level_{level+1}'\n\n        # Only check where child exists\n        valid_rows = taxonomy_df[taxonomy_df[child_col].notna()]\n\n        if valid_rows.empty:\n            continue\n\n        child_counts = valid_rows.groupby(parent_col)[child_col].nunique()\n\n        too_few = (child_counts < 3).sum()\n        too_many = (child_counts > 20).sum()\n\n        if too_few > 0:\n            issues.append(f\"Level {level} has {too_few} parents with < 3 children\")\n        if too_many > 0:\n            issues.append(f\"Level {level} has {too_many} parents with > 20 children\")\n\n    return issues\n```\n\n### 8. Rebalancing Strategy\n```python\ndef rebalance_subcategories(parent, children, min_count=3, max_count=20):\n    \"\"\"Rebalance children to meet size requirements.\"\"\"\n    n = len(children)\n\n    if n < min_count:\n        # Too few: merge with sibling or parent\n        return None  # Signal to merge\n\n    if n > max_count:\n        # Too many: create intermediate groupings\n        # Group by common prefix or semantic similarity\n        groups = create_semantic_groups(children, target_groups=max_count // 2)\n        return groups\n\n    return children\n```\n\n## Output Structure\n\n### Full Taxonomy CSV Format\n```\nsource,category_path,depth,unified_level_1,unified_level_2,unified_level_3,unified_level_4,unified_level_5\namazon,Electronics > Computers > Laptops,3,Electronics,Computers,Laptops,,\ngoogle,Electronics > Computers > Desktop,3,Electronics,Computers,Desktop,,\n```\n\n### Hierarchy CSV Format\n```\nunified_level_1,unified_level_2,unified_level_3,unified_level_4,unified_level_5\nElectronics,Computers,Laptops,,\nElectronics,Computers,Desktop,,\nElectronics,Mobile Devices,Smartphones,,\n```\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1637,"cache_read_input_tokens":29918,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1637,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"cd7bc42e-d3aa-448c-a0c6-ed8448ad6d7a","uuid":"f8cd9957-8b0d-4871-8d79-a5b4b8a5288d"}

Balanced Distribution\n```python\ndef calculate_balance_score(taxonomy_df, level_col):\n    \"\"\"Calculate how balanced the subcategories are under a parent.\"\"\"\n    counts = taxonomy_df.groupby(level_col).size()\n\n    # Coefficient of variation (lower is more balanced)\n    cv = counts.std() / counts.mean()\n\n    # Ideal range check\n    in_range = (counts >= 3) & (counts <= 20)\n    range_compliance = in_range.mean()\n\n    return {'cv': cv, 'range_compliance': range_compliance}\n```\n\n### 3. Source Distribution Across Taxonomy\n```python\ndef check_source_distribution(taxonomy_df, source_col, level_col):\n    \"\"\"Ensure categories from all sources are evenly distributed.\"\"\"\n    source_counts = taxonomy_df.groupby(level_col)[source_col].value_counts()\n    source_coverage = taxonomy_df.groupby(level_col)[source_col].nunique()\n\n    # Ideal: all 3 sources represented in most categories\n    good_coverage = (source_coverage >= 2).mean()\n    return good_coverage\n```\n\n## Implementation Techniques\n\n### 4. Top-Level Category Selection\n```python\n# Standard e-commerce top-level categories (10-20 recommended)\nTOP_LEVEL_CATEGORIES = [\n    'Electronics',\n    'Apparel | Fashion',\n    'Home | Garden',\n    'Sports | Outdoors',\n    'Toys | Games',\n    'Food | Beverages',\n    'Health | Beauty',\n    'Automotive',\n    'Books | Media',\n    'Office | Business',\n    'Pet Supplies',\n    'Baby | Kids',\n    'Industrial | Scientific',\n    'Arts | Crafts',\n    'Tools | Hardware',\n]\n\ndef assign_top_level(category_path, keywords_map):\n    \"\"\"Assign a category path to a top-level category.\"\"\"\n    path_lower = category_path.lower()\n\n    for top_cat, keywords in keywords_map.items():\n        for keyword in keywords:\n            if keyword in path_lower:\n                return top_cat\n\n    return 'Other'\n```\n\n### 5. Subcategory Clustering\n```python\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\ndef cluster_into_subcategories(items, min_size=3, max_size=20):\n    \"\"\"Cluster items into balanced subcategories.\"\"\"\n    n_items = len(items)\n\n    if n_items <= max_size:\n        # No clustering needed, items fit in one group\n        return {'General': items}\n\n    # Target number of clusters\n    target_clusters = max(n_items // 10, 3)  # Aim for ~10 items per cluster\n\n    # Use first word as initial grouping\n    groups = defaultdict(list)\n    for item in items:\n        first_word = item.split()[0] if item.split() else 'Other'\n        groups[first_word].append(item)\n\n    # Merge small groups\n    merged = {}\n    small_items = []\n    for name, group_items in groups.items():\n        if len(group_items) < min_size:\n            small_items.extend(group_items)\n        else:\n            merged[name] = group_items\n\n    if small_items:\n        merged['Other'] = small_items\n\n    return merged\n```\n\n### 6. Depth Adjustment\n```python\ndef adjust_depth(path_parts, target_depth=5):\n    \"\"\"Adjust category path to target depth.\"\"\"\n    current_depth = len(path_parts)\n\n    if current_depth == target_depth:\n        return path_parts\n\n    if current_depth > target_depth:\n        # Truncate to target depth\n        return path_parts[:target_depth]\n\n    if current_depth < target_depth:\n        # Pad with None for missing levels\n        return path_parts + [None] * (target_depth - current_depth)\n```\n\n### 7. Hierarchy Validation\n```python\ndef validate_hierarchy(taxonomy_df):\n    \"\"\"Validate the complete taxonomy hierarchy.\"\"\"\n    issues = []\n\n    # Check Level 1 count\n    l1_count = taxonomy_df['unified_level_1'].nunique()\n    if not (10 <= l1_count <= 20):\n        issues.append(f\"Level 1 has {l1_count} categories (should be 10-20)\")\n\n    # Check subcategory counts at each level\n    for level in range(1, 5):\n        parent_col = f'unified_level_{level}'\n        child_col = f'unified_level_{level+1}'\n\n        # Only check where child exists\n        valid_rows = taxonomy_df[taxonomy_df[child_col].notna()]\n\n        if valid_rows.empty:\n            continue\n\n        child_counts = valid_rows.groupby(parent_col)[child_col].nunique()\n\n        too_few = (child_counts < 3).sum()\n        too_many = (child_counts > 20).sum()\n\n        if too_few > 0:\n            issues.append(f\"Level {level} has {too_few} parents with < 3 children\")\n        if too_many > 0:\n            issues.append(f\"Level {level} has {too_many} parents with > 20 children\")\n\n    return issues\n```\n\n### 8. Rebalancing Strategy\n```python\ndef rebalance_subcategories(parent, children, min_count=3, max_count=20):\n    \"\"\"Rebalance children to meet size requirements.\"\"\"\n    n = len(children)\n\n    if n < min_count:\n        # Too few: merge with sibling or parent\n        return None  # Signal to merge\n\n    if n > max_count:\n        # Too many: create intermediate groupings\n        # Group by common prefix or semantic similarity\n        groups = create_semantic_groups(children, target_groups=max_count // 2)\n        return groups\n\n    return children\n```\n\n## Output Structure\n\n### Full Taxonomy CSV Format\n```\nsource,category_path,depth,unified_level_1,unified_level_2,unified_level_3,unified_level_4,unified_level_5\namazon,Electronics > Computers > Laptops,3,Electronics,Computers,Laptops,,\ngoogle,Electronics > Computers > Desktop,3,Electronics,Computers,Desktop,,\n```\n\n### Hierarchy CSV Format\n```\nunified_level_1,unified_level_2,unified_level_3,unified_level_4,unified_level_5\nElectronics,Computers,Laptops,,\nElectronics,Computers,Desktop,,\nElectronics,Mobile Devices,Smartphones,,\n```\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

5\nMIN_REPRESENTATIVENESS = 0.70\nMAX_SIBLING_OVERLAP = 0.30\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TEXT NORMALIZATION UTILITIES\n# \"\"\"Calculate word overlap ratio between two strings.\"\"\"\n    if not text1 or not text2:\n        return 0.0\n    words1 = set(text1.lower().split())\n    words2 = set(text2.lower().split())\n else None\n        )\n\n    return df\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TOP-LEVEL CATEGORY MAPPING\n#                'electrical', 'building supplies', 'home improvement'],\n}\n\ndef map_to_top_level(category_path):\n    \"\"\"Map a category path to a unified top-level category.\"\"\"\n    if not category_path:\n        return 'Other'\n\n    path_lower = category_path.lower()\n\n    # Score each top-level category\n    scores = {}\n    for unified_cat, keywords in UNIFIED_TOP_LEVEL_KEYWORDS.items():\n        score = 0\n        for kw in keywords:\n            if kw in path_lower:\n                # Weight by position (earlier = higher weight)\n                pos = path_lower.find(kw)\n                weight = max(1, 10 - pos // 20)  # Higher weight for earlier matches\n                score += weight\n        scores[unified_cat] = score\n\n    # Return highest scoring category\n    if max(scores.values()) > 0:\n        return max(scores, key=scores.get)\n\n    return 'Other'\n\n# ============================================================================\n# HIERARCHICAL CLUSTERING\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef get_representative_name(names, min_coverage=0.70):\n    \"\"\"Get a representative name that covers 70%+ of the group.\"\"\"\n    if not names:\n        return ''\n\n    if len(names) == 1:\n        return names[0]\n\n    # Count word frequencies\n    word_counts = Counter()\n    for name in names:\n        words = name.lower().split()\n        word_counts.update(words)\n\n    total = len(names)\n\n    # Find words appearing in 70%+ of names\n    representative_words = []\n    for word, count in word_counts.most_common():\n        if count / total >= min_coverage:\n            representative_words.append(word)\n        if len(representative_words) >= MAX_WORDS_PER_CATEGORY:\n            break\n\n    if representative_words:\n        return format_unified_name(representative_words)\n\n    # Fallback: use most common words\n    common_words = [w for w, c in word_counts.most_common(3)]\n    return format_unified_name(common_words) if common_words else names[0]\n\ndef cluster_categories_by_prefix(categories, level=2):\n    \"\"\"Cluster categories by their common prefix/first word.\"\"\"\n    clusters = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for cat in categories:\n        if not cat:\n            continue\n        # Use first 1-2 words as cluster key\n        words = cat.split()\n        if len(words) >= 2:\n            key = ' '.join(words[:2])\n        else:\n            key = words[0] if words else 'Other'\n        clusters[key.lower()].append(cat)\n\n    return dict(clusters)\n\ndef merge_small_clusters(clusters, min_size=3):\n    \"\"\"Merge clusters that are too small.\"\"\"\n    large_clusters = {}\n    small_items = []\n\n    for name, items in clusters.items():\n        if len(items) >= min_size:\n            large_clusters[name] = items\n        else:\n            small_items.extend(items)\n\n    # Try to merge small items into existing clusters\n    if small_items and large_clusters:\n        # Add to \"Other\" or most similar cluster\n        if 'other' in large_clusters:\n            large_clusters['other'].extend(small_items)\n        else:\n            large_clusters['Other'] = small_items\n    elif small_items:\n        large_clusters['Other'] = small_items\n\n    return large_clusters\n\ndef split_large_clusters(clusters, max_size=20):\n    \"\"\"Split clusters that are too large.\"\"\"\n    result = {}\n\n    for name, items in clusters.items():\n        if len(items) <= max_size:\n            result[name] = items\n        else:\n            # Sub-cluster by second word\n            sub_clusters = defaultdict(list)\n            for item in items:\n                words = item.split()\n                if len(words) >= 2:\n                    key = words[1]\n                else:\n                    key = 'general'\n                sub_clusters[key.lower()].append(item)\n\n            # Add sub-clusters with parent prefix\n            for sub_name, sub_items in sub_clusters.items():\n                full_name = f\"{name}_{sub_name}\"\n                result[full_name] = sub_items\n\n    return result\n\n# build_unified_taxonomy(df):\n    \"\"\"Build the unified 5-level taxonomy.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Building unified taxonomy...\")\n\n    # Step 1: Assign top-level categories\n    print(\"  Assigning  df['unified_level_3'] = None\n    df['unified_level_4'] = None\n    df['unified_level_5'] = None\n\n    for top_cat in df['unified_level_1'].unique():\n        mask = df['unified_level_1'] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Cluster level 2 categories\n        clusters = cluster_categories_by_prefix(level2_cats)\n        clusters = merge_small_clusters(clusters, MIN_SUBCATEGORIES)\n        clusters = split_large_clusters(clusters, MAX_SUBCATEGORIES)\n\n        # Create mapping from original to unified\n        level2_mapping = {}\n        for cluster_name, items in MIN_SUBCATEGORIES).sum()\n        too_many = (child_counts > MAX_SUBCATEGORIES).sum()\n\n        print(f\"  Level {level} -> {level+1}: min={child_counts.min()}, max={child_counts.max()}, mean={child_counts.mean():.1f}\")\n\n        if too_few > 0:\n            issues.append(f\"Level {level} has {too_few} parents with < {MIN_SUBCATEGORIES} children\")\n        if too_many > 0:\n {top_cat}: {dist_str}\")\n\n    # Check sibling overlap\n    print(\"\\n  Checking sibling word overlap...\")\n    for level in range(2, 6):\n        parent_col = f'unified_level_{level - 1}'\n  208→\n   209→def get_representative_name(names, min_coverage=0.70):\n   210→    \"\"\"Get a representative name that covers 70%+ of the group.\"\"\"\n   211→    if not names:\n   212→        return 279→\n   280→def split_large_clusters(clusters, max_size=20):\n   281→    \"\"\"Split clusters that are too large.\"\"\"\n   282→    result = {}\n   283→\n   284→    for name, items in get_representative_name(names, min_coverage=0.70):\n    \"\"\"Get a representative name that covers 70%+ of the group.\"\"\"\n    if not names:\n        return ''\n\n    if len(names) == 1:\n           else:\n                    key = 'general'\n                sub_clusters[key.lower()].append(item)\n\n            # Add sub-clusters with parent prefix\n            for sub_name, sub_items matching.\"\"\"\n   281→    if not items:\n   282→        return {}\n   283→\n   284→    items = list(set(items))  # Deduplicate\n   285→    groups = defaultdict(list)\n   286→    used = set()\n CLUSTERING\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef fuzzy_group_similar(items, threshold=80):\n    \"\"\"Group similar items using fuzzy matching.\"\"\"\n    if not items:\n        return {}\n\n    items = list(set(items))  # Deduplicate\n    groups = defaultdict(list)\n    used = set()\n\n    for item in sorted(items, key=len, reverse=True):  # Process    return dict(groups)\n\ndef get_representative_name(names, min_coverage=0.70):\n    \"\"\"Get a representative name that covers 70%+ of the group.\"\"\"\n    if not names:\n        return ''\n    if len(names) == 1:\n        return names[0]\n\n    # Count word frequencies across all names\n    word_counts = Counter()\n    names_with_word = defaultdict(set)\n\n    for name in names:\n        words    representative_words = []\n    for word, count in word_counts.most_common():\n        if count / total >= min_coverage:\n            representative_words.append(word)\n        if resolve_sibling_overlap(siblings_dict, max_overlap=0.30):\n    \"\"\"Resolve high word overlap between sibling categories.\"\"\"\n    if len(siblings_dict) < 2:\n        return siblings_dict\n\n    names = list(siblings_dict.keys())\n    resolved = {}\n    used_names = set()\n\n    for name in names:\n        new_name = name\n\n        # Check against already resolved names\n        for existing UNIFIED TAXONOMY BUILDING\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef build_unified_taxonomy(df):\n    \"\"\"Build the unified 5-level taxonomy.\"\"\"\n validate_taxonomy(df):\n    \"\"\"Validate the unified taxonomy meets requirements.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nValidating taxonomy...\")\n    issues = []\n\n    # Check top-level count\n    top_count = df['unified_level_1'].nunique()\n    print(f\"  Top-level categories: {top_count}\")\n    if top_count < MIN_TOP_LEVEL:\n        issues.append(f\"Too few top-level categories: {top_count} < MAX_SUBCATEGORIES).sum()\n\n        print(f\"  Level {level} -> {level+1}: min={child_counts.min()}, max={child_counts.max()}, mean={child_counts.mean():.1f}\")\n\n        if too_few > 0:\n            issues.append(f\"Level {level}: {too_few} parents with <{MIN_SUBCATEGORIES} children\")\n        if too_many > 0:\n            issues.append(f\"Level {level}: {too_many} parents with get_cluster_name(items, min_coverage=0.70):\n    \"\"\"Get representative name for a cluster.\"\"\"\n    if not items:\n        return 'General'\n    if len(items) == 1:\n        return format_category_name(items[0])\n\n    # Count words\n    word_counts = Counter()\n    for item in items:\n        for word in tokenize(item):\n            word_counts[word] += 1\n\n    total = adaptively.\"\"\"\n    if not items:\n        return {}\n\n    items = list(set(items))\n    n = len(items)\n\n    # If small enough, return single cluster\n    if n <= max_size:\n        name = get_cluster_name(items)\n        return {name: items}\n\n    # Group by first significant word\n    groups = defaultdict(list)\n    for item in items:\n        words = tokenize(item)\n        key = words[0] if words else 'other'\n        groups[key].append(item)\n\n    result = {}\n    overflow = []\n\n    for key, group in groups.items():\n        if len(group) < min_size:\n            overflow.extend(group)\n        elif len(group) <= max_size:\n            name = get_cluster_name(group)\n            result[name] = group\n        else:\n            # Split large group by second word\n            subgroups = defaultdict(list)\n            for item in group:\n                words = tokenize(item)\n                subkey = words[1] if len(words) > 1 else 'main'\n                subgroups[subkey].append(item)\n\n            for subkey, subgroup in subgroups.items():\n                if len(subgroup) >= min_size:\n                    name = get_cluster_name(subgroup)\n                    if name in result:\n                        name = f\"{name} ({subkey.title()})\"\n                    result[name] = subgroup\n                else:\n                    overflow.extend(subgroup)\n\n    # Handle overflow\n    if overflow:\n        if len(overflow) >= min_size:\n            # Try to group similar items\n            name = get_cluster_name(overflow)\n            result[name] = overflow\n        elif result:\n            # Add to largest existing cluster\n            largest_key = max(result.keys(), key=lambda k: len(result[k]))\n            result[largest_key].extend(overflow)\n        else:\n            result['General'] = overflow\n\n    # Final cleanup: merge remaining small clusters\n    final = {}\n    small = []\n    for name, group in result.items():\n        if len(group) >= min_size:\n            final[name] = group\n        else:\n            small.extend(group)\n\n    if small:\n        if len(small) >= min_size:\n            name = get_cluster_name(small)\n            final[name if name else 'Other'] = small\n        elif final:\n            largest = max(final.keys(), key=lambda k: len(final[k]))\n            final[largest].extend(small)\n\n    return final if final else {'General': items}\n\ndef ensure_unique_names(clusters, parent_name=None):\n    \"\"\"Ensure cluster names are unique and don't overlap with parent.\"\"\"\n    result = {}\n    used = set()\n\n    for name, items in clusters.items():\n        # Remove parent words if needed\n        if parent_name:\n            clean = remove_parent_words(parent_name, name)\n            if clean and len(clean) > 2:\n                name = clean\n\n        # Re-format name\n        name = format_category_name(name)\n        if not {counter}\"\n            counter += 1\n\n        result[name] = items\n        used.add(name)\n\n    return result\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TAXONOMY BUILDING\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef build_taxonomy(df):\n    \"\"\"Build the unified 5-level taxonomy.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nBuilding unified taxonomy...\")\n\n    # Level 1: Top-level categories\n    print(\"  Level 1: Assigning top-level categories...\")\n    df['unified_level_1'] = df[f'unified_level_{i}'] = None\n\n    # Build levels 2-5 hierarchically\n    for level in range(2, 6):\n        print(f\"  Level {level}: Building...\")\n        orig_col = f'orig_level_{level}'\n        unified_col = f'unified_level_{level}'\n        parent_col = f'unified_level_{level - 1}'\n\n        if orig_col not in df.columns:\n            continue\n\n        parents_processed = 0\n        for parent_val in df[parent_col].dropna().unique():\n            mask = df[parent_col] == parent_val\n            orig_cats = df.loc[mask, orig_col].dropna().unique().tolist()\n\n            if not orig_cats:\n                continue\n\n            # Adaptive clustering\n            min_sz = MIN_SUBCATEGORIES if level <= 3 else 1\n            clusters = adaptive_cluster(orig_cats, items in clusters.items():\n                for item in items:\n                    mapping[item] = cluster_name\n\n            # Apply mapping\n            df.loc[mask, unified_col] = df.loc[mask, orig_col].map(mapping)\n            parents_processed += 1\n\n        print(f\"    Processed {parents_processed} parent categories\")\n\n    return df\n\n# ============================================================================\n# VALIDATION\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef validate(df):\n    \"\"\"Validate taxonomy meets requirements.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nValidating taxonomy...\")\n    issues = []\n\n    # Top-level count\n    top_count = df['unified_level_1'].nunique()\n    print(f\"  Top-level categories: {top_count}\")\n    if not (MIN_TOP_LEVEL <= top_count <= MAX_TOP_LEVEL):\n        issues.append(f\"Top-level count {top_count} not in [{MIN_TOP_LEVEL}, {MAX_TOP_LEVEL}]\")\n\n    # Subcategory counts per level\n    for level in range(1, 5):\n        parent_col = f'unified_level_{level}'\n        child_col = f'unified_level_{level + 1}'\n\n        valid = df[df[child_col].notna()]\n        if valid.empty:\n            continue\n\n        counts = valid.groupby(parent_col)[child_col].nunique()\n        too_few = (counts < MIN_SUBCATEGORIES).sum()\n 253→def get_cluster_name(items, min_coverage=0.70):\n   254→    \"\"\"Get representative name for a cluster.\"\"\"\n   255→    if not items:\n   256→        return 'General'\n   257→    if len(items)    words2 = set(tokenize(text2))\n    if not words1 or not words2:\n        return 0.0\n    intersection = words1 & words2\n    min_len = min(len(words1), len(words2))\n    return len(intersection) / min_len if min_len > 0 else 0.0\n\ndef remove_parent_words(parent, child):\n    \"\"\"Remove words from child that appear in parent.\"\"\"\n    if not parent or not child:\n        return child\n path_lower = category_path.lower()\n    scores = {}\n\n    for cat_name, keywords in TOP_LEVEL_MAP.items():\n        score = 0\n        for kw in keywords:\n            if kw in path_lower:\n                pos = path_lower.find(kw)\n                # Higher weight for early matches\n                weight = max(1, 20 - pos // 10)\n                if path_lower.startswith(kw):\n                    weight += 15\n                score += weight\n        scores[cat_name] = score\n\n    if max(scores.values()) > 0:\n        return max(scores, key=scores.get)\n    return 'Other'\n\n# ============================================================================\n# CLUSTERING\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef get_cluster_name(items, min_coverage=0.70):\n    \"\"\"Get representative name for a cluster.\"\"\"\n    if not items:\n        return 'General'\n    if len(items) == 1:\n        return format_category_name(items[0])\n\n    # Count words\n    word_counts = Counter()\n    for item in items:\n        for word in tokenize(item):\n            word_counts[word] += 1\n\n    total = len(items)\n\n    # Find representative words (70%+ coverage)\n    rep_words = [w for w, c in word_counts.most_common()\n                 if c / total >= min_coverage][:3]\n\n    if rep_words:\n        return format_category_name(' '.join(rep_words))\n\n    # Fallback: most common words\n    common = [w for w, _ in word_counts.most_common(2)]\n    if common:\n        return format_category_name(' '.join(common))\n\n    # Last resort: shortest item\n    return format_category_name(min(items, key=len))\n\ndef adaptive_cluster(items, min_size=3, max_size=20):\n    \"\"\"Create balanced clusters adaptively.\"\"\"\n    if not items:\n        return {}\n\n    items = list(set(items))\n    n = len(items)\n\n    # If small enough, return single cluster\n    if n <= max_size:\n        name = get_cluster_name(items)\n        return {name: items}\n\n    # Group by first significant word\n    groups = defaultdict(list)\n    for item in items:\n        words = tokenize(item)\n        key = words[0] if words else 'other'\n        groups[key].append(item)\n\n    result = {}\n    overflow = []\n\n    for key, group in groups.items():\n        if len(group) < min_size:\n            overflow.extend(group)\n        elif len(group) <= max_size:\n            name = get_cluster_name(group)\n            result[name] = group\n        else:\n            # Split large group by second word\n            subgroups = defaultdict(list)\n            for item in group:\n                words = tokenize(item)\n                subkey = words[1] if len(words) > 1 else 'main'\n                subgroups[subkey].append(item)\n\n            for subkey, subgroup in subgroups.items():\n                if len(subgroup) >= min_size:\n                    name = get_cluster_name(subgroup)\n                    if name in result:\n                        name = f\"{name} ({subkey.title()})\"\n                    result[name] = subgroup\n                else:\n                    overflow.extend(subgroup)\n\n    # Handle overflow\n    if overflow:\n        if len(overflow) >= min_size:\n            # Try to group similar items\n            name = get_cluster_name(overflow)\n            result[name] = overflow\n        elif result:\n            # Add to largest existing cluster\n            largest_key = max(result.keys(), key=lambda k: len(result[k]))\n            result[largest_key].extend(overflow)\n        else:\n            result['General'] = overflow\n\n    # Final cleanup: merge remaining small clusters\n    final = {}\n    small = []\n    for name, group in result.items():\n        if len(group) >= min_size:\n            final[name] = group\n        else:\n            small.extend(group)\n\n    if small:\n        if len(small) >= min_size:\n            name = get_cluster_name(small)\n            final[name if name else 'Other'] = small\n        elif final:\n            largest = max(final.keys(), key=lambda k: len(final[k]))\n            final[largest].extend(small)\n\n    return final if final else {'General': items}\n\ndef ensure_unique_names(clusters, parent_name=None):\n    \"\"\"Ensure cluster names are unique and don't overlap with parent.\"\"\"\n    result = {}\n    used = set()\n\n    for name, items in clusters.items():\n        # Remove parent words if needed\n        if parent_name:\n            clean = remove_parent_words(parent_name, name)\n            if clean and len(clean) > 2:\n                name = clean\n\n        # Re-format name\n        name = format_category_name(name)\n        if not name:\n            name = get_cluster_name(items)\n\n        # Ensure uniqueness\n        original = name\n        counter = 1\n        while name in used:\n            name = f\"{original} {counter}\"\n            counter += 1\n\n        result[name] = items\n        used.add(name)\n\n    return result\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TAXONOMY BUILDING\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef build_taxonomy(df):\n    \"\"\"Build the unified 5-level taxonomy.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nBuilding unified taxonomy...\")\n\n    # Level 1: Top-level categories\n    print(\"  Level 1: Assigning top-level categories...\")\n    df['unified_level_1'] = df[f'unified_level_{i}'] = None\n\n    # Build levels 2-5 hierarchically\n    for level in range(2, 6):\n        print(f\"  Level {level}: Building...\")\n        orig_col = f'orig_level_{level}'\n        unified_col = f'unified_level_{level}'\n        parent_col = f'unified_level_{level - 1}'\n\n        if orig_col not in df.columns:\n            continue\n\n        parents_processed = 0\n        for parent_val in df[parent_col].dropna().unique():\n            mask = df[parent_col] == parent_val\n            orig_cats = df.loc[mask, orig_col].dropna().unique().tolist()\n\n            if not orig_cats:\n                continue\n\n            # Adaptive clustering\n            min_sz = MIN_SUBCATEGORIES if level <= 3 else 1\n            clusters = adaptive_cluster(orig_cats, items in clusters.items():\n                for item in items:\n                    mapping[item] = cluster_name\n\n            # Apply mapping\n            df.loc[mask, unified_col] = df.loc[mask, orig_col].map(mapping)\n            parents_processed += 1\n\n        print(f\"    Processed {parents_processed} parent categories\")\n\n    return df\n\n# ============================================================================\n# VALIDATION\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef validate(df):\n    \"\"\"Validate taxonomy meets requirements.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nValidating taxonomy...\")\n    issues = []\n\n    # Top-level count\n    top_count = df['unified_level_1'].nunique()\n    print(f\"  Top-level categories: {top_count}\")\n    if not (MIN_TOP_LEVEL <= top_count <= MAX_TOP_LEVEL):\n        issues.append(f\"Top-level count {top_count} not in [{MIN_TOP_LEVEL}, {MAX_TOP_LEVEL}]\")\n\n    # Subcategory counts per level\n    for level in range(1, 5):\n        parent_col = f'unified_level_{level}'\n        child_col = f'unified_level_{level + 1}'\n\n        valid = df[df[child_col].notna()]\n        if valid.empty:\n            continue\n\n        counts = valid.groupby(parent_col)[child_col].nunique()\n        too_few = (counts < MIN_SUBCATEGORIES).sum()\n        too_many = (counts > MAX_SUBCATEGORIES).sum()\n\n        print(f\"  Level {level}->{level+1}: min={counts.min()}, max={counts.max()}, mean={counts.mean():.1f}\")\n\n        if too_few > 0:\n            issues.append(f\"Level {level}: {too_few} parents with <{MIN_SUBCATEGORIES} children\")\n        if too_many > 0:\n            issues.append(f\"Level {level}: {too_many} parents with >{MAX_SUBCATEGORIES} children\")\n\n    # Source distribution\n    print(\"\\n  Source distribution:\")\n    for top in sorted(df['unified_level_1'].unique()):\n        subset = df[df['unified_level_1'] == top]\n        dist = subset['source'].value_counts(normalize=True)\n        dist_str = ', '.join([f\"{s}: {v:.0%}\" for s, v in dist.items()])\n        print(f\"    {top}: {dist_str}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n  Issues found: {len(issues)}\")\n    for issue in issues[:10]:\n        print(f\"    - {issue}\")\n\n    return issues\n\n# \"\"\"Save output files.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nSaving output files...\")\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n\n    # Compute unified depth\n    def unified_depth(row):\n        d = 0\n    if not items:\n        return {}\n\n    items = list(set(items))  # Deduplicate\n    groups = defaultdict(list)\n    used = set()\n\n    for item in sorted(items, key=len, reverse=True):  # Process validate_taxonomy(df):\n    \"\"\"Validate the unified taxonomy meets requirements.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nValidating taxonomy...\")\n    issues = []\n\n    # Check top-level count\n    top_count = ============================================================================\n\ndef cluster_for_balance(items, min_size=3, max_size=20):\n    \"\"\"Cluster items to meet balance constraints.\"\"\"\n    if not items:\n        return {}\n\n    items = list(set(items))  # Dedupe\n    n = len(items)\n\n    # Single cluster if small enough\n    if n <= max_size:\n        name = groups[key].append(item)\n\n    result = {}\n    overflow = []\n\n    for key, group in groups.items():\n        if len(group) < min_size:\n            overflow.extend(group)\n        elif len(group) defaultdict(list)\n            for item in group:\n                words = tokenize(item)\n                subkey = words[1] if len(words) > 1 else 'main'\n                subgroups[subkey].append(item)\n\n            for subkey, subgroup in subgroups.items():\n                if len(subgroup) < min_size:\n                    overflow.extend(subgroup)\n 1\n                    while name in result:\n                        name = f\"{base_name} {counter}\"\n                        counter += 1\n                    result[name] = subgroup\n                else:\n                    # Still too large - chunk it\n                    chunk_size = max_size // 2\n                    for i in range(0, len(subgroup), chunk_size):\n                        chunk = subgroup[i:i+chunk_size]\n                        if chunk:\n                            name = create_cluster_name(chunk)\n                            base = name\n                            c = 1\n                            while name in result:\n                                name = f\"{base} {c}\"\n                                c += 1\n                            result[name] = chunk\n\n    # Handle overflow\n    if overflow:\n        if len(overflow) >= min_size:\n            name = create_cluster_name(overflow)\n            if name in result:\n                name =      result[smallest_key].extend(overflow)\n        else:\n            name = create_cluster_name(overflow)\n            result[name] = overflow\n\n    # Final merge of small clusters\n    final = {}\n    small = []\n    for name, group in result.items():\n        if len(group) >= min_size:\n            final[name] = group\n        else:\n            small.extend(group)\n\n    if small:\n ============================================================================\n\ndef cluster_for_balance(items, min_size=3, max_size=20):\n    \"\"\"Cluster items to meet balance constraints.\"\"\"\n      result[smallest_key].extend(overflow)\n        else:\n            name = create_cluster_name(overflow)\n            result[name] = overflow\n\n    # Final merge of small clusters\n    final = ============================================================================\n\ndef cluster_for_balance(items, min_size=3, max_size=20):\n    \"\"\"Cluster items to meet balance constraints.\"\"\"\n subgroups[subkey].append(item)\n\n            for subkey, subgroup in subgroups.items():\n                if len(subgroup) < min_size:\n                    overflow.extend(subgroup)\n 1\n                    while name in result:\n                        name = f\"{base_name} {counter}\"\n                        counter += 1\n                    result[name] = subgroup\n {df['unified_1'].nunique()} categories\")\n\n    # Level 2: Cluster orig_2 within each L1\n    print(\"  Level 2: Clustering...\")\n    for l1 in df['unified_1'].unique():\n        mask = {df['unified_1'].nunique()} categories\")\n\n    # Level 2: Cluster orig_2 within each L1\n    print(\"  Level 2: Clustering...\")\n    for l1 in df['unified_1'].unique():\n        mask =
